<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD&quot;,&quot;跨域与多场景推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;模型合并&quot;,&quot;锐度感知优化&quot;,&quot;LoRA 重参数化&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2026/cross_domain_multi_scenario/sharpness_aware_model_merging_with_salience_recovery_for_llm_based_cross_do/">Sharpness-aware Model Merging with Salience Recovery for LLM-based Cross-Domain Sequential Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="跨域与多场景推荐">Cross</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用锐度感知平坦极小值对齐 + 重尾重参数化恢复显著偏好信号，破解 LLM 跨域序列推荐模型合并中的参数冲突与多域饱和瓶颈。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2026&quot;,&quot;多模态推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;Hybrid-Query Connector&quot;,&quot;可靠性门控&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2026/multimodal_rec/text_guided_visual_representation_learning_for_robust_multimodal_e_commerce/">Text-Guided Visual Representation Learning for Robust Multimodal E-Commerce Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多模态推荐与内容理解">MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以结构化元数据初始化语义查询、双流解耦视觉证据并用可靠性双门控校准，在 134K 全库 I2I 检索上 H@100 相对提升 6.04%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2026&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;不确定性建模&quot;,&quot;GRPO偏好优化&quot;,&quot;置信度对齐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2026/generative_rec/uncertainty_aware_generative_recommendation/">Uncertainty-aware Generative Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将生成置信度与样本难度转化为GRPO奖励与梯度权重，并显式对齐置信度token，稳定训练并支持风险感知推荐。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;基准审计&quot;,&quot;推理期干预&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2026/llm_rec/whose_name_comes_up_ii_benchmarking_and_intervention_based_auditing_of_llm_/">Whose Name Comes Up? II: Benchmarking and Intervention-Based Auditing of LLM-Based Scholar Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出 LLMScholarBench，联合审计 22 个 LLM 在 5 类学者推荐任务、3 类推理期干预下的技术质量与社会代表性权衡。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;RAG&quot;,&quot;模型更新策略&quot;,&quot;兴趣探索&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/llm_rec/balancing_fine_tuning_and_rag_a_hybrid_strategy_for_dynamic_llm_recommendat/">Balancing Fine-tuning and RAG: A Hybrid Strategy for Dynamic LLM Recommendation Updates</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以十亿用户短视频兴趣探索系统为案例，量化对比微调与RAG的更新代价/敏捷性，提出月度微调+周内RAG刷新的混合更新策略，线上A/B显著提升满意度。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;画像生成&quot;,&quot;用户研究&quot;,&quot;因果去偏&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/llm_rec/biases_in_llm_generated_musical_taste_profiles_for_recommendation/">Biases in LLM-Generated Musical Taste Profiles for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>通过 64 人用户研究揭示 LLM 生成音乐品味画像存在流派/国别系统性偏差，且用户认同度与下游推荐指标仅弱相关。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2025&quot;,&quot;冷启动&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;通用文本嵌入&quot;,&quot;零样本迁移&quot;,&quot;有效维度&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/cold_start_rec/do_we_really_need_specialization_evaluating_generalist_text_embeddings_for_/">Do We Really Need Specialization? Evaluating Generalist Text Embeddings for Zero-Shot Recommendation and Search</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>实证通用文本嵌入(GTE)零样本即超越领域微调模型，并以PCA有效维度揭示空间利用率与压缩收益。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;离线评测&quot;,&quot;LLM-as-a-Judge&quot;,&quot;用户画像&quot;,&quot;播客推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/llm_rec/evaluating_podcast_recommendations_with_profile_aware_llm_as_a_judge/">Evaluating Podcast Recommendations with Profile-Aware LLM-as-a-Judge</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用90天收听历史蒸馏自然语言用户画像，驱动零样本LLM做剧集级与模型级离线评判，47人用户研究中与人工判断高度一致。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys&quot;,&quot;冷启动&quot;,&quot;评测与元评估&quot;,&quot;LLM-as-Evaluator&quot;,&quot;Serendipity&quot;,&quot;用户模拟&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/cold_start_rec/exploring_the_potential_of_llms_for_serendipity_evaluation_in_recommender_s/">Exploring the Potential of LLMs for Serendipity Evaluation in Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 LLM 模拟用户对推荐结果做惊喜度（serendipity）打分，在淘宝与 MovieLens 两个真实用户调研数据集上做元评估，最优配置 Pearson 相关达 21.5%，显著超越传统代理指标。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;异构用户建模&quot;,&quot;域跷跷板&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/llm_rec/heterogeneous_user_modeling_for_llm_based_recommendation/">Heterogeneous User Modeling for LLM-based Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="跨域与多场景推荐">Cross</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用压缩提示+[USER]锚点token将跨域异构行为压成单一用户表征，并以域重要性平滑加权缓解域跷跷板，六域全库排序R@10最高提升约30%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2025&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;实体引导兴趣建模&quot;,&quot;对比预训练&quot;,&quot;双塔用户编码器&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/collaborative_filtering/ip2_entity_guided_interest_probing_for_personalized_news_recommendation/">IP2: Entity-Guided Interest Probing for Personalized News Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以实体-标题对比预训练探测新闻内实体焦点，并用跨注意力双塔用户编码器建模新闻间实体引导兴趣，MIND-large AUC 达 72.06%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2025&quot;,&quot;多模态推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;TransE 平移建模&quot;,&quot;多模态交叉注意力&quot;,&quot;自然语言查询推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/multimodal_rec/just_ask_for_music_jam_multimodal_and_personalized_natural_language_music_r/">Just Ask for Music (JAM): Multimodal and Personalized Natural Language Music Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多模态推荐与内容理解">MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将查询建模为用户到物品的向量平移，用交叉注意力聚合音频/歌词/CF 多模态，在 JAMSessions 全库排序上 NDCG@10 相对 TalkRec 提升约 105%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2025&quot;,&quot;多模态推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;LLM-as-Annotator&quot;,&quot;知识蒸馏&quot;,&quot;个性化受限召回&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/multimodal_rec/llm_powered_nuanced_video_attribute_annotation_for_enhanced_recommendations/">LLM-Powered Nuanced Video Attribute Annotation for Enhanced Recommendations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多模态推荐与内容理解">MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 Gemini 多模态 LLM 做短视频“氛围”属性标注，经金/银集评估与蒸馏扩量，接入个性化受限召回，线上参与度 +0.49%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;反事实解释&quot;,&quot;流行度去偏&quot;,&quot;LLM 画像过滤&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/mitigating_popularity_bias_in_counterfactual_explanations_using_large_langu/">Mitigating Popularity Bias in Counterfactual Explanations using Large Language Models</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 LLM 生成用户画像并贪心剔除“出格”历史项，作为 ACCENT 反事实解释的即插即用预处理，使解释流行度分布更贴合用户偏好。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2025&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;LLM 反思 (Reflection)&quot;,&quot;Contextual Bandit 视角选择&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/sequential_rec/more_a_mixture_of_reflectors_framework_for_large_language_model_based_seque/">MoRE: A Mixture of Reflectors Framework for Large Language Model-Based Sequential Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用显式/隐式/协同三视角离线反思 + 元反思器自改进与 Contextual Bandit 在线选视角，LLM 序列推荐全库候选下 NDCG@10 最高提升 10.1%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2025&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;时间间隔建模&quot;,&quot;LLM4Rec&quot;,&quot;Interval-Infused Attention&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/sequential_rec/not_just_what_but_when_integrating_irregular_intervals_to_llm_for_sequentia/">Not Just What, But When: Integrating Irregular Intervals to LLM for Sequential Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将不规则时间间隔经 MLP 编码与 Interval-Infused Attention 注入 LLaMA-2，配合选项化提示，在 3 个 Amazon 数据集上 HR@1 平均提升 4.4%，并提出“间隔冷启动”新评测视角。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;模型反演攻击&quot;,&quot;LLM4Rec 隐私&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/privacy_risks_of_llm_empowered_recommender_systems_an_inversion_attack_pers/">Privacy Risks of LLM-Empowered Recommender Systems: An Inversion Attack Perspective</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首次系统研究 LLM 推荐系统的 logits 反演攻击，提出相似度引导精炼，可恢复 65% 交互物品与 87% 年龄性别。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2025&quot;,&quot;CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;System-2 反思精炼&quot;,&quot;LLM 知识增强&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/ctr_feature_interaction/r4ec_a_reasoning_reflection_and_refinement_framework_for_recommendation_sys/">R4ec: A Reasoning, Reflection, and Refinement Framework for Recommendation Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 actor/reflection 双小模型迭代反思精炼 LLM 知识，注入 CTR 主干，AUC 相对提升 2.5%+，线上收入 +2.2%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2025&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;语义ID&quot;,&quot;联合搜索推荐&quot;,&quot;RQ-KMeans&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/generative_rec/semantic_ids_for_joint_generative_search_and_recommendation/">Semantic IDs for Joint Generative Search and Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>系统对比任务专属与跨任务语义ID构建策略，发现多任务双塔嵌入+统一RQ-KMeans语义ID在联合搜索推荐中取得最佳权衡。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;用户模拟&quot;,&quot;偏好优化&quot;,&quot;推理期搜索&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/agent_conversational_rec/usb_rec_an_effective_framework_for_improving_conversational_recommendation_/">USB-Rec: An Effective Framework for Improving Conversational Recommendation Capability of Large Language Model</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用带标签用户模拟器自动构造偏好对做 RL 训练，再在推理期用内部用户模拟器+树搜索自增强，iEval 全面超越 SOTA。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2025&quot;,&quot;多模态推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;Visual Grounding&quot;,&quot;LLM 文本增强&quot;,&quot;CLIP 对比微调&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/multimodal_rec/vl_clip_enhancing_multimodal_recommendations_via_visual_grounding_and_llm_a/">VL-CLIP: Enhancing Multimodal Recommendations via Visual Grounding and LLM-Augmented CLIP Embeddings</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多模态推荐与内容理解">MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 Grounding DINO 裁剪商品主体 + LLM 自反思改写商品描述，再微调 CLIP 双塔，Walmart 线上 CTR +18.6%、GMV +4.0%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2025&quot;,&quot;跨域与多场景推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;跨域知识蒸馏&quot;,&quot;多任务排序&quot;,&quot;零样本迁移&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/cross_domain_multi_scenario/zero_shot_cross_domain_knowledge_distillation_a_case_study_on_youtube_music/">Zero-shot Cross-domain Knowledge Distillation: A Case study on YouTube Music</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="跨域与多场景推荐">Cross</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 YouTube 视频大教师零样本蒸馏到低流量 YouTube Music 双排序模型，离线 AUC 微增但线上新发内容互动 +11.39%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;智能体即用户&quot;,&quot;三方博弈&quot;,&quot;跨平台代理&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2026/agent_conversational_rec/a_position_paper_on_recommender_systems_in_the_era_of_autonomous_agents/">A Position Paper on Recommender Systems in the Era of Autonomous Agents</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出人-智能体-平台三方混合推荐生态，将智能体视为用户侧独立代理，重构任务定义、评估范式与四大研究方向。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2026&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;合成多轮对话&quot;,&quot;智能体自改进&quot;,&quot;LLM-as-a-Judge&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2026/agent_conversational_rec/bootstrapping_conversational_recommendation_agents_at_spotify_synthetic_dat/">Bootstrapping Conversational Recommendation Agents At Spotify: Synthetic Data Generation and Self-Improvement Loops</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用多轮合成对话+方差归因自改进循环冷启动对话推荐智能体，线上A/B提升14%收听与5%周活。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys&quot;,&quot;社交与群组推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;LLM-as-Judge&quot;,&quot;社会选择聚合&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2026/social_group_rec/consensus_vs_dissent_dynamic_llm_modeling_of_subjective_preferences_in_grou/">Consensus vs. Dissent: Dynamic LLM Modeling of Subjective Preferences in Group Recommenders</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="社交与群组推荐">Social</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用人类问卷+DeepSeek-V3.1 蒸馏 CoT 微调 Llama/OLMo 作实时判断模型，动态挑选社会选择聚合策略，用户研究(n=284)中公平与共识得分最高。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回/特征增强&quot;,&quot;小语言模型&quot;,&quot;属性偏好预测&quot;,&quot;时序用户建模&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2026/llm_rec/deepaffinity_long_term_aspect_preference_prediction_in_ecommerce_using_smal/">DeepAffinity: Long-Term Aspect Preference Prediction in eCommerce using Small Language Models</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 135M 小模型+结构化字符串化+多任务预测头，从时序行为预测品牌/尺码/颜色等商品属性偏好，Fashion 微 F1 0.34 超 Gemini 零样本与 3B SFT。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;货架生成&quot;,&quot;语义ID生成式检索&quot;,&quot;LLM-as-a-Judge&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2026/generative_rec/hypothesis_driven_shelf_generation_for_personalised_recommendation/">Hypothesis-Driven Shelf Generation for Personalised Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用自然语言货架假设替代固定模板，解耦规划与目录履约，生成式检索+货架对齐显著提升个性化供给</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;反事实数据增强&quot;,&quot;长尾罕见药推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2026/llm_rec/improving_rare_medication_recommendation_with_counterfactual_data_augmentat/">Improving Rare Medication Recommendation with Counterfactual Data Augmentation and Large Language Models</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 LLM 反事实数据增强 + 病历摘要指令微调 + 逐药自回归生成，缓解罕见药长尾失效，Group-1 Jaccard 最高 +30.2%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;Inverse Theory of Mind&quot;,&quot;Persona Synthesis&quot;,&quot;LLM 反事实推理&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2026/agent_conversational_rec/inverse_theory_of_mind_modeling_for_content_recommendation_from_web_browsin/">Inverse Theory of Mind Modeling for Content Recommendation: From Web Browsing to Dynamic Intelligent Interfaces</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 LLM 从浏览轨迹反向推断信念并合成多假设人格画像，在 OPeRA 上超越真实人格基线并驱动 VisionOS 空间界面。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;RLVR&quot;,&quot;推理崩溃&quot;,&quot;人格路由&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2026/llm_rec/llm_reasoning_for_subjective_tasks_failure_modes_mitigation_and_dynamic_rea/">LLM Reasoning for Subjective Tasks: Failure Modes, Mitigation, and Dynamic Reasoning Routing</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>揭示主观验证任务上显式推理与标准 RLVR 会引发推理崩溃，提出条件长度惩罚奖励与人格路由蓝图，Netflix 四任务 macro-F1 最高回升至 0.851。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;评测基准&quot;,&quot;LLM-as-a-Judge&quot;,&quot;响应生成评估&quot;,&quot;对话音乐推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2026/agent_conversational_rec/llm_as_a_judge_for_evaluating_system_responses_in_conversational_music_reco/">LLM-as-a-Judge for Evaluating System Responses in Conversational Music Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首个以 400 条专家标注实证 LLM-as-a-Judge 在对话音乐推荐响应评估中与人类判断的对齐度，并给出条件化与规模选择指南。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;用户画像蒸馏&quot;,&quot;异步在线推理&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2026/llm_rec/llm_based_user_personas_for_recommendations_at_scale/">LLM-Based User Personas for Recommendations at Scale</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>工业级实时生成自然语言用户兴趣画像，蒸馏+异步推理支撑十亿用户在线服务，A/B 测试观看时长 +0.04%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;LLM Agent 编排&quot;,&quot;实体接地&quot;,&quot;反思式重试&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2026/agent_conversational_rec/melo_a_production_llm_powered_music_recommendation_agent/">Melo: A Production LLM-Powered Music Recommendation Agent</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>网易云音乐生产级 LLM 音乐推荐 Agent，以五节点确定性状态图 + 推理时实体接地 + 反思式重试解决实体幻觉与长尾塌缩，线上 A/B 留存提升超 2pp。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;成员推理攻击&quot;,&quot;In-Context Learning&quot;,&quot;隐私泄露&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/membership_inference_attacks_on_in_context_examples_in_llm_based_recommende/">Membership Inference Attacks on In-Context Examples in LLM-based Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首次针对 ICL 式 LLM 推荐系统提出 ItemMem 与 RecInertia 两种成员推理攻击，黑盒下攻击优势最高近 1.0，现有提示抽取防御基本失效。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;可解释推荐&quot;,&quot;离线候选池选择&quot;,&quot;成对排序学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2026/llm_rec/pairwise_ranking_outperforms_single_action_rl_for_offline_explanation_selec/">Pairwise Ranking Outperforms Single-Action RL for Offline Explanation Selection: A Practical Lesson</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将 LLM 解释生成离线化为冻结候选池，用 CPU 端 LambdaRank 逐候选打分选择，F1=0.500 超越 G-Refer/XRec 且延迟&lt;100ms。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;LLM Agent&quot;,&quot;Tool Learning&quot;,&quot;记忆反思机制&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2026/llm_rec/personalized_recommendation_tool_learning_via_autonomous_language_agents/">Personalized Recommendation Tool Learning via Autonomous Language Agents</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>让 LLM 智能体做个性化工具选择、传统推荐模型做全库打分，以架构设计绕开幻觉与上下文长度限制，Full-Ranking 下 R@10 最高提升 68.8%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2026&quot;,&quot;多任务学习&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;位置偏置&quot;,&quot;置换一致性&quot;,&quot;LLM 列表重排&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2026/multi_task_reranking/position_bias_undermines_preference_consistency_in_listwise_llm_based_reran/">Position Bias Undermines Preference Consistency in Listwise LLM-Based Reranking</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将 LLM 列表重排的候选置换视为诱导偏好系统，提出 PPI/GPI/LOC 三层一致性度量，揭示有效性、曝光均衡与置换一致性三者可背离。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;渐进式后训练&quot;,&quot;强化微调对齐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2026/llm_rec/progressive_alignment_of_recommender_foundation_model_through_multi_phase_p/">Progressive Alignment of Recommender Foundation Model through Multi-Phase Post-Training</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将推荐基础模型后训练拆为 LP→FFT→RFT 三阶段，用稠密隐式反馈训练策略、稀疏业务指标仅训练奖励模型，离线 Rank NDCG 0.437→0.463，线上全漏斗指标显著优于非 FM 基线。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;流式用户画像&quot;,&quot;GRPO 强化学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2026/llm_rec/recap_feedback_driven_streaming_semantic_user_profiles_for_short_video_reco/">RECAP: Feedback-Driven Streaming Semantic User Profiles for Short-Video Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将流式结构化用户画像视为有界记忆状态，用 LLM 语义差分+确定性状态机维护，并以 LLM 判官清洗隐式反馈、双塔评估器打分作 GRPO 奖励闭环优化画像更新器。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;LLM Agent&quot;,&quot;奖励工程&quot;,&quot;自动机器学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2026/llm_rec/self_evolving_recommendation_system_end_to_end_autonomous_model_optimizatio/">Self-Evolving Recommendation System: End-To-End Autonomous Model Optimization With LLM Agents</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 Gemini 双智能体（快循环提名+慢循环上线）自动改写优化器/架构/奖励函数，在 YouTube 线上 A/B 取得显著北极星指标提升。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;混合检索&quot;,&quot;自适应路由&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2026/collaborative_filtering/smart_llm_augmented_hybrid_retrieval_for_dynamic_product_ads/">SMART: LLM-Augmented Hybrid Retrieval for Dynamic Product Ads</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>揭示规则查询擅长 BM25 重定向、LLM 查询擅长 ANN 拓客的检索分解，用输出审计门控仅路由 10% 用户，线上转化率 +27.6%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;查询改写&quot;,&quot;路径式端到端检索&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2026/llm_rec/spear_selection_aware_personalized_end_to_end_adaptive_rewriting_and_retrie/">SPEAR: Selection-aware Personalized End-to-end Adaptive Rewriting and Retrieval for Community Search</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以梯度隔离双嵌入+乘性门控+动态改写选择器统一查询改写与检索，离线点击召回@10提升99.5%，线上QVCTR+0.259%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;模态偏置&quot;,&quot;结构保持投影&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2026/llm_rec/structure_preserving_projection_for_mitigating_modality_bias_in_llm_based_s/">Structure-Preserving Projection for Mitigating Modality Bias in LLM-Based Sequential Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用余弦相似度保持与序列对比保持双损失约束协同嵌入投影，缓解 LLM 推荐中的模态偏置，20 候选协议下 HR@1 最高提升约 12pt。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;CoT 难样本挖掘&quot;,&quot;KTO 行为对齐&quot;,&quot;知识蒸馏&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2025/llm_rec/adore_autonomous_domain_oriented_relevance_engine_for_e_commerce/">ADORE: Autonomous Domain-Oriented Relevance Engine for E-commerce</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 CoT-LLM 自动挖掘难样本、KTO 对齐用户行为、错误类型感知合成对抗样本，再以关键属性显式蒸馏到轻量 BERT，离线 F1 85.45%、线上 CTR +1.24%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2025&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;Agentic Feedback Loop&quot;,&quot;LLM User Simulation&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2025/agent_conversational_rec/agentic_feedback_loop_modeling_improves_recommendation_and_user_simulation/">Agentic Feedback Loop Modeling Improves Recommendation and User Simulation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用记忆驱动的推荐智能体-用户智能体多轮反馈闭环，同时提升下一物品推荐与用户行为模拟，并验证不放大流行度/位置偏差。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2025&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;查询改写&quot;,&quot;LLM 指令微调&quot;,&quot;意图一致性过滤&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2025/sequential_rec/ai_guided_accelerator_for_search_experience/">AI Guided Accelerator For Search Experience</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>挖掘电商查询转移链并做意图一致性过滤，再用 Solar-10B 指令微调生成多样化替代收敛查询，线上 CTR +32.2%、转化 +38.3%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2025&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;零空间学习&quot;,&quot;语言嵌入融合&quot;,&quot;冷启动&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2025/sequential_rec/alphafuse_learn_id_embeddings_for_sequential_recommendation_in_null_space_o/">AlphaFuse: Learn ID Embeddings for Sequential Recommendation in Null Space of Language Embeddings</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将语言嵌入 SVD 分解为语义丰富行空间与语义稀疏零空间，在裁剪后的零空间内学习 ID 嵌入，无需适配器即插即用地融合语义与协同信号。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2025&quot;,&quot;跨域与多场景推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;跨域序列推荐&quot;,&quot;LLM 语义嵌入&quot;,&quot;分层用户画像&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2025/cross_domain_multi_scenario/bridge_the_domains_large_language_models_enhanced_cross_domain_sequential_r/">Bridge the Domains: Large Language Models Enhanced Cross-domain Sequential Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="跨域与多场景推荐">Cross</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 LLM 语义嵌入+分层画像桥接跨域物品与用户偏好，破解重叠困境与转移复杂性，三数据集 H@10 最高提升 31.7%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2025&quot;,&quot;时空轨迹与POI推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;多智能体协作&quot;,&quot;候选空间收缩&quot;,&quot;反向推理微调&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2025/poi_spatiotemporal_rec/comapoi_a_collaborative_multi_agent_framework_for_next_poi_prediction_bridg/">CoMaPOI: A Collaborative Multi-Agent Framework for Next POI Prediction Bridging the Gap Between Trajectory and Language</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="时空轨迹与 POI 本地生活推荐">POI</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 Profiler/Forecaster/Predictor 三智能体把数值轨迹转语言画像、动态收缩候选集并反向推理微调，三数据集全指标提升 4%–12%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2025&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;知识图谱增强推荐&quot;,&quot;LLM 语义嵌入&quot;,&quot;检索增强表征&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2025/graph_rec/comprehending_knowledge_graphs_with_large_language_models_for_recommender_s/">Comprehending Knowledge Graphs with Large Language Models for Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 LLM 理解 KG 局部子图并构建语义物品图做全局检索增强，离线预计算语义嵌入后接入 LightGCN，全库排序下全面超越 12 个基线。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;由粗到细检索&quot;,&quot;子图检索&quot;,&quot;LLM推理&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2025/llm_rec/corona_a_coarse_to_fine_framework_for_graph_based_recommendation_with_large/">CORONA: A Coarse-to-Fine Framework for Graph-based Recommendation with Large Language Models</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 LLM 做偏好/意图推理在交互图上逐级缩小候选子图，再由 GNN 精排，全库排序下 Recall 平均相对提升 18.6%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;数据集记忆&quot;,&quot;流行度偏置&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2025/llm_rec/do_llms_memorize_recommendation_datasets_a_preliminary_study_on_movielens_1/">Do LLMs Memorize Recommendation Datasets? A Preliminary Study on MovieLens-1M</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用少样本提示探测 GPT/Llama 对 MovieLens-1M 的物品、用户与交互记忆覆盖率，揭示记忆量与推荐性能强相关且放大流行度偏置。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;离线知识生成&quot;,&quot;推测解码&quot;,&quot;检索池分组&quot;,&quot;松弛验证&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2025/llm_rec/efficiency_unleashed_inference_acceleration_for_llm_based_recommender_syste/">Efficiency Unleashed: Inference Acceleration for LLM-based Recommender Systems with Speculative Decoding</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将检索式推测解码引入 LLM 离线知识生成，用定制检索池与松弛验证实现 3-5× 无损加速，线上省 67% 算力。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2025&quot;,&quot;CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;知识蒸馏&quot;,&quot;LLM增强CTR&quot;,&quot;伪孪生网络&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2025/ctr_feature_interaction/elec_efficient_large_language_model_empowered_click_through_rate_prediction/">ELEC: Efficient Large Language Model-Empowered Click-Through Rate Prediction</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>伪孪生增益/朴素双网络+分数级CLID与表示级MSE双蒸馏，在线仅跑表格DCNv2，AUC较FLIP提升0.0020/0.0034。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;无ID推荐&quot;,&quot;文本投毒攻击&quot;,&quot;多智能体协作&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/id_free_not_risk_free_llm_powered_agents_unveil_risks_in_id_free_recommende/">ID-Free Not Risk-Free: LLM-Powered Agents Unveil Risks in ID-Free Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>揭示无ID推荐器可被LLM多智能体改写文本攻击，提出TextSimu攻击与RewriteDetection检测，Hit@50最高提升数倍。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回+重排&quot;,&quot;Chain-of-Thought&quot;,&quot;位置偏差 MAPB&quot;,&quot;偏好抽取&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2025/llm_rec/improving_llm_powered_recommendations_with_personalized_information/">Improving LLM-powered Recommendations with Personalized Information</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将用户偏好与物品感知两条 CoT 显式抽取并注入 CRM 召回与 LLM 重排，Leave-One-Out 全库排序下 Hit@10 最高提升约 11%，并显著降低位置偏差。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;意图解耦表征&quot;,&quot;跨模态对齐&quot;,&quot;动量蒸馏&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2025/llm_rec/intent_representation_learning_with_large_language_model_for_recommendation/">Intent Representation Learning with Large Language Model for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 LLM 思维链抽取文本意图，与交互意图双塔对齐+动量蒸馏匹配，作为模型无关插件在三个数据集上稳定提升 Recall/NDCG。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2025&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;Interest Unit&quot;,&quot;语义对齐检索&quot;,&quot;多兴趣建模&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2025/collaborative_filtering/ira_adaptive_interest_aware_representation_and_alignment_for_personalized_m/">IRA: Adaptive Interest-aware Representation and Alignment for Personalized Multi-interest Retrieval</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用可累积/剪枝的文本化 Interest Unit 表征多兴趣，语义对齐替代点击信号做 ANN 召回，离线 H@20 提升 20%，线上点击 +5.4%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;双曲空间推荐&quot;,&quot;元优化两阶段训练&quot;,&quot;层级信息对齐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2025/llm_rec/large_language_models_enhanced_hyperbolic_space_recommender_systems/">Large Language Models Enhanced Hyperbolic Space Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>HyperLLM 用 LLM 生成层级标签与偏好摘要，经元优化两阶段训练将结构/语义层级信息注入双曲推荐，Recall@20 最高提升 41.7%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2025&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;线性物品-物品模型&quot;,&quot;知识蒸馏&quot;,&quot;闭式解&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2025/sequential_rec/linear_item_item_model_with_neural_knowledge_for_session_based_recommendati/">Linear Item-Item Model with Neural Knowledge for Session-based Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将神经会话模型的转移知识蒸馏为物品-物品矩阵正则项，与线性自蒸馏相似度统一闭式求解，R@20 最高提升 14.78% 且推理 FLOPs 降低 813 倍。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;Truth Decay&quot;,&quot;LLM 生成假新闻&quot;,&quot;新闻推荐可信性&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/llm_generated_fake_news_induces_truth_decay_in_news_ecosystem_a_case_study_/">LLM-Generated Fake News Induces Truth Decay in News Ecosystem: A Case Study on Neural News Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>构建约 7.5 万条人机混合新闻推荐数据集，模拟 LLM 生成假新闻在候选、历史、训练集三阶段渗透，揭示真实新闻排序优势被侵蚀的 Truth Decay 现象并以困惑度给出解释。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;掩码Softmax损失&quot;,&quot;自适应温度&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2025/llm_rec/msl_not_all_tokens_are_what_you_need_for_tuning_llm_as_a_recommender/">MSL: Not All Tokens Are What You Need for Tuning LLM as a Recommender</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出掩码 Softmax 损失屏蔽虚构物品 token，并配自适应温度策略，四数据集 NDCG@10 平均提升 42.24%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;层次化压缩&quot;,&quot;Patch 预训练&quot;,&quot;长序列建模&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2025/llm_rec/multi_grained_patch_training_for_efficient_llm_based_recommendation/">Multi-Grained Patch Training for Efficient LLM-based Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用层次化 item/session patch 压缩长交互历史，两阶段训练让 LLM 理解压缩表征，同 token 预算下 HR@20 最高提升 72%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2025&quot;,&quot;CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;查询改写&quot;,&quot;多目标偏好对齐&quot;,&quot;竞价词生成&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2025/ctr_feature_interaction/multi_objective_aligned_bidword_generation_model_for_e_commerce_search_adve/">Multi-objective Aligned Bidword Generation Model for E-commerce Search Advertising</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用判别器提供相关性/真实性/CPM三维奖励，扩展MODPO做多目标偏好对齐，驱动LLM生成高商业价值竞价词，线上广告收入+2.13%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2025&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;无序集合标识符&quot;,&quot;稀疏注意力掩码&quot;,&quot;查询引导同时生成&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2025/generative_rec/order_agnostic_identifier_for_large_language_model_based_generative_recomme/">Order-agnostic Identifier for Large Language Model-based Generative Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出无序集合标识符范式，用CF+语义多token集合表示物品并同时生成，T5上Recall@10平均提升约50%，推理提速8-18倍。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;MK-MMD 特征核对齐&quot;,&quot;三专家解耦&quot;,&quot;灾难性遗忘&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2025/llm_rec/pre_train_align_and_disentangle_empowering_sequential_recommendation_with_l/">Pre-train, Align, and Disentangle: Empowering Sequential Recommendation with Large Language Models</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 MK-MMD 特征核做推荐锚定对齐，再以三专家频率门控解耦，Full-Ranking 下冷物品 HR@10 提升最高 462%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;GFlowNet&quot;,&quot;过程监督&quot;,&quot;流行度去偏&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2025/llm_rec/process_supervised_llm_recommenders_via_flow_guided_tuning/">Process-Supervised LLM Recommenders via Flow-guided Tuning</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 GFlowNet 将物品级奖励分解为 token 级过程奖励，替代 SFT 的似然最大化，在 Amazon 三数据集上同时提升准确率、公平性与多样性。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2025&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;端到端对话式检索&quot;,&quot;对话式商品搜索&quot;,&quot;数据集构建&quot;,&quot;多任务基准&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2025/agent_conversational_rec/pscon_product_search_through_conversations/">PSCon: Product Search Through Conversations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出人-人教练式采集协议构建双市场双语真实 CPS 数据集 PSCon，定义六子任务流水线并给出 Transformer 统一基准模型。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;评论偏好画像&quot;,&quot;推理蒸馏&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2025/llm_rec/review_driven_personalized_preference_reasoning_with_large_language_models_/">Review-driven Personalized Preference Reasoning with Large Language Models for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用评论抽取偏好画像并蒸馏逐步推理，评分预测与重排双任务均超 CF 与 LLM 基线，解释性显著提升</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2025&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;用户模拟&quot;,&quot;生成式奖励模型&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2025/agent_conversational_rec/search_based_interaction_for_conversation_recommendation_via_generative_rew/">Search-Based Interaction For Conversation Recommendation via Generative Reward Model Based Simulated User</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用生成式奖励模型构建无标签模拟用户，通过属性评论与生成式打分驱动束搜索交互，INSPIRED 上 Recall@10 相对最优基线提升 83%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2025&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;用户世界模型&quot;,&quot;扩散模型用户画像&quot;,&quot;布朗桥反馈预判&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2025/agent_conversational_rec/simulating_before_planning_constructing_intrinsic_user_world_model_for_user/">Simulating Before Planning: Constructing Intrinsic User World Model for User-Tailored Dialogue Policy Planning</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用扩散模型刻画用户画像、布朗桥预判用户反馈，构建内在用户世界模型以定制对话策略，P4G/ESConv 上 SR 显著提升。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;数据合成与基准构建&quot;,&quot;Knowledge-Grounded Generation&quot;,&quot;Persona-Based Personalization&quot;,&quot;Synthetic Query Benchmark&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2025/llm_rec/synthtrips_a_knowledge_grounded_framework_for_benchmark_query_generation_fo/">SynthTRIPs: A Knowledge-Grounded Framework for Benchmark Query Generation for Personalized Tourism Recommenders</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 PersonaHub 人格 + 可持续性过滤器 + KB 接地，让 LLM 批量合成个性化城市游查询，4,604 条查询经 J-LLM 与双专家双轨验证。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2025&quot;,&quot;跨域与多场景推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;LoRA 动态集成&quot;,&quot;跨域序列推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2025/cross_domain_multi_scenario/x_cross_dynamic_integration_of_language_models_for_cross_domain_sequential_/">X-Cross: Dynamic Integration of Language Models for Cross-Domain Sequential Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="跨域与多场景推荐">Cross</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>逐层动态集成多个 LoRA 源域语言模型，仅用 25% 参数与 50-75% 更少微调数据即达/超 LoRA 跨域序列推荐效果。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;Customer Persona&quot;,&quot;Random Walk Approximation&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2025/llm_rec/you_are_what_you_bought_generating_customer_personas_for_e_commerce_applica/">You Are What You Bought: Generating Customer Personas for E-commerce Applications</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 LLM 标注少量原型用户、随机游走传播生成可读人物画像，并以 RevAff 近似加速，画像注入 GCN 推荐最高提升 12%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;级联生成&quot;,&quot;RAG 约束解码&quot;,&quot;教师学生蒸馏&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/generative_rec/a_cascaded_generative_approach_for_e_commerce_recommendations/">A Cascaded Generative Approach for e-Commerce Recommendations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出级联生成式货架构建框架：LLM1 生成投放主题、LLM2 经 RAG 约束生成检索关键词，教师-学生蒸馏降本，线上购物车添加 +2.7%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;多模态推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;MM-LLM 字幕生成&quot;,&quot;语义特征注入&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/multimodal_rec/a_general_framework_for_multimodal_llm_based_multimedia_understanding_in_la/">A General Framework for Multimodal LLM-Based Multimedia Understanding in Large-Scale Recommendation Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多模态推荐与内容理解">MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 1.5B LLaMA2+BLIP-2 将图像转字幕并 token 化为类别特征注入 DLRM 精排，离线 AUC +0.35%、线上 +0.02%，延迟中性。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;嵌入各向异性&quot;,&quot;线性自编码器谱收缩&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/llm_rec/ace_anisotropy_controllable_embedding_for_llm_enhanced_sequential_recommend/">ACE: Anisotropy-Controllable Embedding for LLM-enhanced Sequential Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用线性自编码器的谱收缩算子连续调控 LLM 物品嵌入的各向异性，在保持语义层级的同时缓解几何失衡，Full-Ranking 下 Recall@20 最高提升 12.4%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;多源 Grounding&quot;,&quot;双意图消歧&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/llm_rec/agentic_multi_source_grounding_for_enhanced_query_intent_understanding_a_do/">Agentic Multi-Source Grounding for Enhanced Query Intent Understanding: A DoorDash Case Study</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用目录实体检索+Agentic 网页搜索双源 grounding 替代 winner-takes-all 分类，输出有序双意图并由可插拔消歧层裁决，长尾意图准确率 +13.0pp。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;Beam Search 对齐&quot;,&quot;训练-推理对齐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/llm_rec/bear_towards_beam_search_aware_optimization_for_recommendation_with_large_l/">BEAR: Towards Beam-Search-Aware Optimization for Recommendation with Large Language Models</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>揭示 SFT 训练与 beam search 推理的剪枝不一致，提出 token 级 top-B 必要条件的可微正则 BEAR，平均提升 12.50% 且训练开销几乎不变。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;Best-of-N Alignment&quot;,&quot;贝叶斯动态估计&quot;,&quot;GRPO&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/llm_rec/beyond_static_best_of_n_bayesian_list_wise_alignment_for_llm_based_recommen/">Beyond Static Best-of-N: Bayesian List-wise Alignment for LLM-based Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用贝叶斯后验融合静态先验与在线 rollout 证据，构建自演化 BoN 目标，突破静态对齐的性能上界，NDCG@5 相对提升 23.1%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;双层优化&quot;,&quot;梯度手术&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/generative_rec/bi_level_optimization_for_generative_recommendation_bridging_tokenization_a/">Bi-Level Optimization for Generative Recommendation: Bridging Tokenization and Generation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将分词器与推荐器建模为双层优化，用元学习+梯度手术让推荐损失直接监督RQ-VAE码本，Full-Ranking下全面超越TIGER/LETTER/ETEGRec。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;双通道图学习&quot;,&quot;频率感知门控融合&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/graph_rec/dcgl_dual_channel_graph_learning_with_large_language_models_for_knowledge_a/">DCGL: Dual-Channel Graph Learning with Large Language Models for Knowledge-Aware Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>双通道解耦 LLM 语义与 ID 行为信号，多级对比对齐 + 频率感知门控融合，四数据集全库排序全面超越 SOTA。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;双意图空间表征重构&quot;,&quot;对齐-均匀性优化&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/collaborative_filtering/diaurec_dual_intent_space_representation_optimization_for_recommendation/">DIAURec: Dual-Intent Space Representation Optimization for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以原型意图与分布意图重构表征，用对齐-均匀性+粗细粒度匹配联合优化，Amazon-book R@20 相对提升 10.6%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;LLM推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;序数语义锚定&quot;,&quot;强度感知对齐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/llm_rec/every_preference_has_its_strength_injecting_ordinal_semantics_into_llm_base/">Every Preference Has Its Strength: Injecting Ordinal Semantics into LLM-Based Recommenders</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将显式评分建模为LLM词表中的序数语义锚点，以强度感知对齐交互表征，在序列推荐Hit@1与成对偏好准确率上显著超越CF-LLM混合基线。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;隐式推理&quot;,&quot;因子解耦&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/llm_rec/factorized_latent_reasoning_for_llm_based_recommendation/">Factorized Latent Reasoning for LLM-based Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将 LLM 隐式推理拆为 K 个解耦偏好因子并配 GRPO 对齐，全库约束解码下 N@5 最高提升 10.26%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;知识缺口估计&quot;,&quot;选择性提示增强&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/llm_rec/filling_the_gaps_selective_knowledge_augmentation_for_llm_recommenders/">Filling the Gaps: Selective Knowledge Augmentation for LLM Recommenders</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出 CKP 比较式知识探测量化 LLM 对物品的协同知识缺口，仅对知识贫乏物品选择性注入属性与参考物品，免训练提升 Recall@1 与长尾覆盖。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;LLM 上下文增强&quot;,&quot;地理-时间嵌入&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/sequential_rec/from_time_and_place_to_preference_llm_driven_geo_temporal_context_in_recomm/">From Time and Place to Preference: LLM-Driven Geo-Temporal Context in Recommendations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 LLM 从时间戳+粗粒度位置生成地理-时间上下文嵌入，以特征融合或辅助对齐损失注入 SASRec，并提出轻量信息量诊断测试。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;分面搜索&quot;,&quot;GRPO偏好对齐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/llm_rec/genfacet_end_to_end_generative_faceted_search_via_multi_task_preference_ali/">GenFacet: End-to-End Generative Faceted Search via Multi-Task Preference Alignment in E-Commerce</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将电商分面搜索重构为分面生成与查询改写两个耦合生成任务，经蒸馏+SFT+GRPO 对齐搜索效用，线上分面 CTR 相对提升 42.0%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;Page-wise NTP&quot;,&quot;GRPO-SR 偏好对齐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/generative_rec/genrec_a_preference_oriented_generative_framework_for_large_scale_recommend/">GenRec: A Preference-Oriented Generative Framework for Large-Scale Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以 Page-wise NTP 解一对多歧义、线性 Token Merger 压缩 2× 输入、GRPO-SR 混合奖励对齐偏好，京东线上点击 +9.5%、成交 +8.7%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;RAG&quot;,&quot;共购关系&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/llm_rec/itemrag_item_based_retrieval_augmented_generation_for_llm_based_recommendat/">ItemRAG: Item-Based Retrieval-Augmented Generation for LLM-Based Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将 RAG 从粗粒度用户历史检索转向细粒度物品级检索，融合共购与语义信息，标准与冷启动场景全面超越用户级 RAG 基线。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;参数级适配&quot;,&quot;双视图混合专家&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/llm_rec/l2rec_towards_dual_view_understanding_of_llms_for_personalized_recommendati/">L2Rec: Towards Dual-View Understanding of LLMs for Personalized Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以参数级双视图 LoRA-MoE 替代输入/输出级融合，个性化路由对齐行为与语义视图，四数据集 N@10 提升 3.87%–8.02%，线上 CTR +9.24%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;个性化软指令&quot;,&quot;拉格朗日约束优化&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/llm_rec/lwgr_lagrangian_constrained_personalized_world_knowledge_for_generative_rec/">LWGR: Lagrangian-Constrained Personalized World Knowledge for Generative Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用并行码本生成用户个性化软指令抽取 LLM 世界知识，并以拉格朗日原始-对偶约束控制知识融合，Full-Ranking 下最高相对提升 11.23%，线上广告收入 +1.35%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;用户模拟&quot;,&quot;双过程融合&quot;,&quot;决策策略挖掘&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/agent_conversational_rec/meituan_merchant_business_diagnosis_via_policy_guided_dual_process_user_sim/">Meituan Merchant Business Diagnosis via Policy-Guided Dual-Process User Simulation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>从行为轨迹挖掘决策策略作为共享对齐层，锚定 LLM 推理与 GBDT 拟合双过程分支，群体仿真误差降至 8.80%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;多模态推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;MLLM 偏好推理&quot;,&quot;物品图精修&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/multimodal_rec/mllmrec_a_preference_reasoning_paradigm_with_graph_refinement_for_multimoda/">MLLMRec: A Preference Reasoning Paradigm with Graph Refinement for Multimodal Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多模态推荐与内容理解">MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 MLLM 推理用户偏好画像替代噪声聚合初始化，并以阈值去噪+共现增强精修物品图，三数据集 11/12 指标 SOTA。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回+精排+重排&quot;,&quot;表征压缩&quot;,&quot;模块化解耦&quot;,&quot;中间层表征优势&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/llm_rec/modular_representation_compression_adapting_llms_for_efficient_and_effectiv/">Modular Representation Compression: Adapting LLMs for Efficient and Effective Recommendations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>揭示 LLM 中间层表征优势 (MRA) 并用模块化解耦压缩修复，128 维表征在 CTR/重排/召回全面超越基线，线上 eCPM +2.82%</p>
</dd>
