<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;多模态偏好对齐&quot;,&quot;难度感知DPO&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/llm_rec/multimodal_large_language_models_with_adaptive_preference_optimization_for_/">Multimodal Large Language Models with Adaptive Preference Optimization for Sequential Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多模态推荐与内容理解">MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>面向 MLLM 序列推荐，用难度感知重加权 DPO + 高斯噪声正则分布优化，缓解样本难度失衡与跨模态语义偏差，三数据集 NDCG@3 提升 7.4%–11.0%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;多任务学习&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;排列不变性&quot;,&quot;列表级重排&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/multi_task_reranking/one_pass_any_order_position_invariant_listwise_reranking_for_llm_based_reco/">One Pass, Any Order: Position-Invariant Listwise Reranking for LLM-Based Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用结构化注意力掩码+共享位置框架在架构层消除候选顺序依赖，单次前向实现近完美排列不变性，nDCG 仅微降。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;LLM推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;分布重塑&quot;,&quot;用户画像&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/llm_rec/promax_exploring_the_potential_of_llm_derived_profiles_with_distribution_sh/">ProMax: Exploring the Potential of LLM-derived Profiles with Distribution Shaping for Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将 LLM 画像视为检索信号而非融合特征，用双路分布重塑（监督+自监督）引导基座模型，LightGCN 上 NDCG@10 最高提升 8.44%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;数据投毒攻击&quot;,&quot;提示蒸馏&quot;,&quot;序列推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/llm_rec/prompt_unknown_promotion_attacks_against_llm_based_sequential_recommender_s/">Prompt-Unknown Promotion Attacks against LLM-based Sequential Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>在系统提示词与受害模型均不可见的全黑盒设定下，用进化式提示蒸馏+代理模型+文本与行为双投毒实现长尾商品推广攻击，Beauty 上 HR@10 提升约 3.9 倍。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;端侧推理&quot;,&quot;意图理解&quot;,&quot;自适应提示构建&quot;,&quot;意图漂移触发&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/llm_rec/recgpt_mobile_on_device_large_language_models_for_user_intent_understanding/">RecGPT-Mobile: On-Device Large Language Models for User Intent Understanding in Taobao Feed Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将 Qwen3-0.6B 量化后部署于手机端，用意图漂移触发机制按需推理生成下一搜索意图，线上 GMV +2.5%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;对话交互&quot;,&quot;用户领域知识估计&quot;,&quot;游戏化数据采集&quot;,&quot;LLM 零样本/少样本&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/agent_conversational_rec/recquest_towards_estimating_user_domain_knowledge_in_conversational_recomme/">RecQuest: Towards Estimating User Domain Knowledge in Conversational Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出游戏化数据采集协议 RecQuest 与知识估计新任务，用客观问卷替代自评建立 ground truth，并给出 LLM 零/少样本与微调基线。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;表征对齐&quot;,&quot;互补性融合&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/llm_rec/rethinking_semantic_collaborative_integration_why_alignment_is_not_enough/">Rethinking Semantic–Collaborative Integration: Why Alignment Is Not Enough</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>论证语义-协同全局对齐假设过强，提出共享+私有潜结构与互补性诊断，最小融合在 Amazon 三域 Full-Ranking 上超越对齐范式最高 41.7%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;混合物品分词&quot;,&quot;指令多任务SFT&quot;,&quot;自适应概率融合&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/generative_rec/sigma_a_semantic_grounded_instruction_driven_generative_multi_task_recommen/">SIGMA: A Semantic-Grounded Instruction-Driven Generative Multi-Task Recommender at AliExpress</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>多视图语义对齐+混合SID/ID分词+指令多任务SFT+三步生成与自适应概率融合，离线HR@10达33.76%，线上GMV+7.84%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;谱域适配器&quot;,&quot;维度坍缩&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/llm_rec/spectran_spectral_aware_transformer_based_adapter_for_llm_enhanced_sequenti/">SpecTran: Spectral-Aware Transformer-based Adapter for LLM-Enhanced Sequential Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用全谱注意力+可学习谱位置编码替代静态SVD截断，缓解LLM语义嵌入投影的维度坍缩，四数据集三骨干平均提升9.17%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;LLM 意图画像&quot;,&quot;不确定性采样&quot;,&quot;自训练伪标签&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/sequential_rec/sprint_scalable_and_predictive_intent_refinement_for_llm_enhanced_session_b/">SPRINT: Scalable and Predictive Intent Refinement for LLM-Enhanced Session-based Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用全局意图池约束 LLM 生成、以预测-纠错回路校验意图，并蒸馏为轻量意图预测器，实现训练选择性调用 LLM、推理零 LLM 依赖的会话推荐。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;用户模拟&quot;,&quot;自动化用户画像生成&quot;,&quot;反事实因果映射&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/agent_conversational_rec/task_aware_automated_user_profile_generation_for_recommendation_simulation_/">Task-Aware Automated User Profile Generation for Recommendation Simulation Using Large Language Models</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出免训练三阶段画像生成框架 APG4RecSim，用语义合并+反事实因果映射将交互历史转为任务对齐画像，在判别/排序/评分三任务上取得最佳综合表现。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;自适应推理&quot;,&quot;推理路由&quot;,&quot;Pareto 前沿&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/llm_rec/think_when_needed_model_aware_reasoning_routing_for_llm_based_ranking/">Think When Needed: Model-Aware Reasoning Routing for LLM-based Ranking</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出轻量即插即用的模型感知推理路由器，在生成前逐实例决定 Think/Non-Think，以 Pareto 前沿策略在降 token 的同时提升排序效用。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;多任务学习&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;稀疏监督对齐&quot;,&quot;在线学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/multi_task_reranking/towards_end_to_end_alignment_of_user_satisfaction_via_questionnaire_in_vide/">Towards End-to-End Alignment of User Satisfaction via Questionnaire in Video Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 LoRA+MoE 独立参数通路隔离稀疏问卷满意度信号，并以在线 DPO 对齐主排序模型，离线 NDCG@5 提升约 3%，线上 Q-Satisfied 提升 0.5–0.9%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;梯度子空间调优&quot;,&quot;零空间投影&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/llm_rec/unifying_search_and_recommendation_in_llms_via_gradient_multi_subspace_tuni/">Unifying Search and Recommendation in LLMs via Gradient Multi-Subspace Tuning</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用梯度多子空间分解+零空间投影替代 LoRA，在统一 S&amp;R 生成式建模中消解任务梯度冲突并保住通用知识，Qilin/Amazon 上大幅超越 SOTA。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;可验证推理&quot;,&quot;隐空间推理&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/llm_rec/verifiable_reasoning_for_llm_based_generative_recommendation/">Verifiable Reasoning for LLM-based Generative Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出 reason-verify-recommend 范式，用混合验证器对隐空间推理逐步审计与修正，四数据集 Recall@10 最高提升约 47%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;个性化查询改写&quot;,&quot;GRPO 风格对齐&quot;,&quot;轻量索引并行召回&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/llm_rec/when_how_to_write_for_personalized_demand_aware_query_rewriting_in_video_se/">When &amp; How to Write for Personalized Demand-aware Query Rewriting in Video Search</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用后验行为挖掘「何时改写」样本、SFT+GRPO 对齐检索索引风格，并以并行轻量召回实现零感知延迟的个性化查询改写。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;偏好引导策略&quot;,&quot;MoE&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/agent_conversational_rec/when_and_how_to_ask_dynamic_preference_elicitation_strategies_for_conversat/">When and How to Ask: Dynamic Preference Elicitation Strategies for Conversational Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出 InPE 标注数据集与 COPE 分层 MoE 架构，显式建模“何时问、怎么问”的阶段感知偏好引导策略，Recall@10 相对最优基线提升 14.6%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TKDD&quot;,&quot;CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;知识蒸馏&quot;,&quot;双流MLP对齐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDD/2026/ctr_feature_interaction/dual_stream_mlp_is_all_you_need_for_ctr_prediction/">Dual-Stream MLP is All You Need for CTR Prediction</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Knowledge Discovery from Data">ACM TKDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用知识蒸馏把复杂显式交叉教师压进纯MLP双流学生，配合隐状态BN与预测对齐，三基准AUC全面SOTA且推理低延迟。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TKDE&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;评测基准&quot;,&quot;语义评估&quot;,&quot;话题标签嵌入&quot;,&quot;同义词库&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDE/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/reval_a_semantic_evaluation_framework_for_hashtag_recommendation/">#REVAL: a semantic evaluation framework for hashtag recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE Transactions on Knowledge and Data Engineering">IEEE TKDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出语义评估框架 #REval，用 BERTag 话题标签嵌入构建同义词库，以 #REval-hit-ratio 替代精确匹配，修正话题标签推荐评估的系统性低估。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TKDE&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;协同嵌入映射&quot;,&quot;两阶段调优&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDE/2025/llm_rec/collm_integrating_collaborative_embeddings_into_large_language_models_for_r/">CoLLM: Integrating Collaborative Embeddings into Large Language Models for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE Transactions on Knowledge and Data Engineering">IEEE TKDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将传统协同过滤模型学到的用户/物品表征经 MLP 映射进 LLM 词嵌入空间，以两阶段调优兼顾暖启动与冷启动，AUC 相对 TALLRec 提升约 8%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TKDE&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;图感知对比学习&quot;,&quot;多跳提示构造&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDE/2025/llm_rec/enhancing_collaborative_semantics_of_language_model_driven_recommendations_/">Enhancing Collaborative Semantics of Language Model-Driven Recommendations via Graph-Aware Learning</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE Transactions on Knowledge and Data Engineering">IEEE TKDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用多跳提示+图感知对比学习把 GNN 聚合意图注入 LLM，在三个 Amazon/Yelp 数据集 Full-Ranking 下 HR@10 最高提升约 15%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TKDE&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;Machine Unlearning&quot;,&quot;LoRA Adapter Aggregation&quot;,&quot;隐私保护&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDE/2025/llm_rec/exact_and_efficient_unlearning_for_large_language_model_based_recommendatio/">Exact and Efficient Unlearning for Large Language Model-based Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE Transactions on Knowledge and Data Engineering">IEEE TKDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首个面向 LLMRec 的精确遗忘框架 APA：语义分片训练子 LoRA、仅重训受影响分片，并以样本自适应参数级聚合实现单次推理，遗忘提速约 4 倍且 AUC 反超全量重训。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TKDE&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;知识蒸馏&quot;,&quot;偏好一致性&quot;,&quot;模型压缩&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDE/2025/llm_rec/preference_consistent_knowledge_distillation_for_recommender_system/">Preference-Consistent Knowledge Distillation for Recommender System</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE Transactions on Knowledge and Data Engineering">IEEE TKDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>揭示特征蒸馏中投影器导致的偏好不一致现象，提出成对/列表两种正则项约束投影器，全库排序下 NDCG@20 最高提升 20%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TKDE&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;向量量化ID分词&quot;,&quot;生成式检索&quot;,&quot;协同知识注入&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDE/2025/generative_rec/tokenrec_learning_to_tokenize_id_for_llm_based_generative_recommendation/">TokenRec: Learning to Tokenize ID for LLM-based Generative Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE Transactions on Knowledge and Data Engineering">IEEE TKDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用掩码K路向量量化把GNN协同表征压成LLM兼容离散token，并以生成式检索替代自回归解码，HR@20平均提升约19%、推理加速超10倍。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TKDE&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;审慎对齐&quot;,&quot;多步推理&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDE/2026/llm_rec/reason4rec_deliberative_user_preference_alignment_of_large_language_models_/">Reason4Rec: Deliberative User Preference Alignment of Large Language Models for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE Transactions on Knowledge and Data Engineering">IEEE TKDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出审慎推荐任务与三专家多步推理框架，用评论监督偏好蒸馏-匹配-预测，评分预测与排序全面领先。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;数据增强&quot;,&quot;LLM4Rec&quot;,&quot;ID-based Recommendation&quot;,&quot;LoRA 微调&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2025/llm_rec/enhancing_id_based_recommendation_with_large_language_models/">Enhancing ID-based Recommendation with Large Language Models</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 LoRA 微调 LLM 仅凭纯 ID 序列生成伪交互数据，经双重过滤后注入任意 ID 基推荐模型，全库排序下平均提升 3%–9%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;联邦序列推荐&quot;,&quot;dX-privacy 序列扰动&quot;,&quot;对比学习知识迁移&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2025/llm_rec/fellas_enhancing_federated_sequential_recommendation_with_llm_as_external_s/">FELLAS: Enhancing Federated Sequential Recommendation with LLM as External Services</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将 LLM 作为外部服务为联邦序列推荐提供物品级与序列级表征，配合 dX-privacy 扰动与对比学习，在 Full-Ranking 下显著超越 FedSeqRec 并逼近集中式训练。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;全链路架构&quot;,&quot;LLM4Rec&quot;,&quot;分类体系&quot;,&quot;综述&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2025/llm_rec/how_can_recommender_systems_benefit_from_large_language_models_a_survey/">How Can Recommender Systems Benefit from Large Language Models: A Survey</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以“WHERE×HOW”双正交视角系统梳理 LLM 融入推荐全链路的五阶段角色与四象限适配范式，并给出效率/效果/伦理三大挑战。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;反事实推断&quot;,&quot;维度坍缩&quot;,&quot;谱编码器&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2025/llm_rec/mitigating_propensity_bias_of_large_language_models_for_recommender_systems/">Mitigating Propensity Bias of Large Language Models for Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用谱条件 iVAE 编码 LLM 侧信息避免维度坍缩，并以反事实推断消除 LLM 倾向性偏差，在 6 个 CF 骨干上全库排序一致提升。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;多域序列推荐&quot;,&quot;文本表征替代ID&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2025/llm_rec/one_model_for_all_large_language_models_are_domain_agnostic_recommendation_/">One Model for All: Large Language Models are Domain-Agnostic Recommendation Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="跨域与多场景推荐">Cross</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 LLM 编码跨域混合标题序列实现域无关推荐，采样 1000 负例评测，SPIAO 上较最优基线相对提升约 19%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;个性化预训练&quot;,&quot;专家发现&quot;,&quot;投票导向任务&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2025/llm_rec/pept_expert_finding_meets_personalized_pre_training/">PEPT: Expert Finding Meets Personalized Pre-training</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将专家历史问答、投票分与专家ID统一进BERT预训练，用问题级MLM与投票预测双任务学习个性化兴趣与专长，六域CQA全库排序MRR全面领先。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;LLM Agent&quot;,&quot;Tool-Augmented Recommendation&quot;,&quot;Conversational Recommendation&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2025/agent_conversational_rec/recommender_ai_agent_integrating_large_language_models_for_interactive_reco/">Recommender AI Agent: Integrating Large Language Models for Interactive Recommendations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以 LLM 为大脑、传统推荐模型为工具，配合候选总线、长短期画像、计划优先执行与反思机制，在 Steam/MovieLens/Beauty 上显著超越 GPT-4 与 Chat-Rec。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;指令微调&quot;,&quot;混合排序&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2025/llm_rec/recranker_instruction_tuning_large_language_model_as_ranker_for_top_k_recom/">RecRanker: Instruction Tuning Large Language Model as Ranker for Top-k Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以自适应用户采样、位置偏移与检索信号增强构建三类排序指令微调 LLM 重排器，并提出混合排序集成，Leave-One-Out 全库排序下 HR/NDCG 最高提升 213%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;提示词个性化&quot;,&quot;多智能体强化学习&quot;,&quot;实例级提示&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2025/llm_rec/reinforced_prompt_personalization_for_recommendation_with_large_language_mo/">Reinforced Prompt Personalization for Recommendation with Large Language Models</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将提示词拆为四类句式模式，用多智能体强化学习为每个用户逐句选择并组合个性化提示，在三个数据集上 NDCG@10 相对任务级提示提升 107%–132%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;行为-文本适配&quot;,&quot;掩码物品文本预测&quot;,&quot;多域预训练&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2025/llm_rec/thoroughly_modeling_multi_domain_pre_trained_recommendation_as_language/">Thoroughly Modeling Multi-domain Pre-trained Recommendation as Language</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将多域序列推荐彻底转为文本生成任务，用五项指标指导行为-文本双向适配，7 数据集微调与零样本均最优，NDCG@1 最高提升 23%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;LLM Agent&quot;,&quot;用户行为模拟&quot;,&quot;信息茧房&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2025/agent_conversational_rec/user_behavior_simulation_with_large_language_model_based_agents/">User Behavior Simulation with Large Language Model based Agents</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出 LLM Agent 用户行为模拟器 RecAgent，以画像-记忆-动作三模块与沙盒环境复现信息茧房与从众现象，行为可信度逼近真人。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;图神经网络推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;符号边重加权&quot;,&quot;LLM 表征增强&quot;,&quot;精度-多样性权衡&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2026/graph_rec/cpgrec_a_balance_oriented_framework_for_personalized_video_game_recommendat/">CPGRec+: A Balance-oriented Framework for Personalized Video Game Recommendations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以 Fisher 分布符号边重加权缓解过平滑、LLM 生成偏好描述精炼表征，在 Steam 双数据集上兼顾精度与多样性。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TORS&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;两步接地&quot;,&quot;全库排序&quot;,&quot;统计信息注入&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TORS/2025/llm_rec/a_bi_step_grounding_paradigm_for_large_language_models_in_recommendation_sy/">A Bi-Step Grounding Paradigm for Large Language Models in Recommendation Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Recommender Systems">ACM TORS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出 BIGRec 两步接地范式：先指令微调 LLM 生成物品语义 token，再以 L2 距离对齐真实物品空间，全库排序下 1024 样本即超越全量训练基线。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TORS&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;音乐推荐评估&quot;,&quot;LLM-as-Judge&quot;,&quot;提示工程&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TORS/2026/llm_rec/music_recommendation_with_large_language_models_challenges_opportunities_an/">Music Recommendation with Large Language Models: Challenges, Opportunities, and Evaluation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Recommender Systems">ACM TORS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>系统梳理 LLM 驱动音乐推荐在用户建模、物品建模与自然语言请求三方面的变革，并提出 NLP 启发的评估分类体系与 MRS 成功/风险维度框架。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WSDM 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;可控多样性推荐&quot;,&quot;任务分解&quot;,&quot;数据增广&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WSDM/2025/llm_rec/dlcrec_a_novel_approach_for_managing_diversity_in_llm_based_recommender_sys/">DLCRec: A Novel Approach for Managing Diversity in LLM-Based Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Web Search and Data Mining">WSDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将多样性可控推荐拆解为类型预测/填充/物品预测三子任务级联，配合两类数据增广，在 Movie/Steam 上 MAE_Cov@10 大幅领先。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WSDM 2025&quot;,&quot;CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;LLM解释生成&quot;,&quot;PPO双奖励对齐&quot;,&quot;LoRA三阶段迭代&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WSDM/2025/ctr_feature_interaction/explainable_ctr_prediction_via_llm_reasoning/">Explainable CTR Prediction via LLM Reasoning</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Web Search and Data Mining">WSDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 PPO 双奖励（LC 用户意图对齐 + IC 与 ID-CTR 模型对齐）把 LLM 解释生成嵌入 CTR 流程，免解释数据集，AUC 最高提升 18.2%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WSDM 2025&quot;,&quot;强化学习与动态探索推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;LLM 偏好蒸馏&quot;,&quot;自适应策略融合&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WSDM/2025/rl_bandit_rec/large_language_model_driven_policy_exploration_for_recommender_systems/">Large Language Model driven Policy Exploration for Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Web Search and Data Mining">WSDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 LLM 蒸馏用户偏好离线预训练 A2C 推荐策略，并提出冻结+可学习双策略自适应在线适配，显著提升冷启动回报与长期收益。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WSDM 2025&quot;,&quot;冷启动&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;LLM 行为模拟&quot;,&quot;耦合漏斗过滤&quot;,&quot;严格冷启动&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WSDM/2025/cold_start_rec/large_language_model_simulator_for_cold_start_recommendation/">Large Language Model Simulator for Cold-Start Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Web Search and Data Mining">WSDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 LLM 模拟冷物品的用户交互行为，并以耦合漏斗（FAISS 过滤 + LLM 精炼）把十亿级候选压到数百，离线冷召回 NDCG 提升 37%+，线上冷启 GMV +16.9%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WSDM 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;方面级语义控制&quot;,&quot;幻觉度量&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WSDM/2026/llm_rec/hadsf_aspect_aware_semantic_control_for_explainable_recommendation/">HADSF: Aspect Aware Semantic Control for Explainable Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Web Search and Data Mining">WSDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>两阶段受控方面词表+动态个性化抽取，提出ADR/OFR幻觉度量，揭示幻觉-性能非单调关系，小模型可媲美大模型</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WSDM 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;端侧推理&quot;,&quot;模型压缩&quot;,&quot;低秩分解&quot;,&quot;序列推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WSDM/2026/llm_rec/on_device_large_language_models_for_sequential_recommendation/">On-Device Large Language Models for Sequential Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Web Search and Data Mining">WSDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>面向端侧序列推荐的 LLM 任务自适应压缩框架 OD-LLM，融合 Token 协方差归一化、SVD 低秩分解与逐层渐进对齐，50% 压缩率下推荐效果无损且推理提速 3.4 倍。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WSDM 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;推荐助手基准&quot;,&quot;复杂查询推理&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WSDM/2026/llm_rec/towards_next_generation_recommender_systems_a_benchmark_for_personalized_re/">Towards Next-Generation Recommender Systems: A Benchmark for Personalized Recommendation Assistant with LLMs</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Web Search and Data Mining">WSDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>构建 RecBench+ 约3万条复杂用户查询基准，覆盖显式/隐式/误导条件与画像偏好，系统评测7个LLM并揭示SFT+RFT两阶段微调最优。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2025&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;用户模拟&quot;,&quot;插件化 Agent&quot;,&quot;数据泄漏抑制&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2025/agent_conversational_rec/a_llm_based_controllable_scalable_human_involved_user_simulator_framework_f/">A LLM-based Controllable, Scalable, Human-Involved User Simulator Framework for Conversational Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以插件管理器分阶段管控 LLM 用户模拟器，通过记忆隔离与偏好二分抑制数据泄漏，在 ReDial/OpenDialKG/MovieLens 上显著提升 CRS 评估可信度。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2025&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;LLM Agent&quot;,&quot;环境模拟器&quot;,&quot;用户行为建模&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2025/agent_conversational_rec/agentsociety_challenge_designing_llm_agents_for_user_modeling_and_recommend/">AgentSociety Challenge: Designing LLM Agents for User Modeling and Recommendation on Web Platforms</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首个 WWW LLM Agent 双赛道竞赛：Yelp/Amazon/Goodreads 三源数据 + 交互式模拟器，295 队参赛，Track1/2 最终阶段提升 9.1%/15.9%，并验证模拟 groundtruth 与真实分布强相关。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;CF-LLM 融合&quot;,&quot;Soft Prompt&quot;,&quot;叙事生成&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2025/llm_rec/beyond_retrieval_generating_narratives_in_conversational_recommender_system/">Beyond Retrieval: Generating Narratives in Conversational Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>构建 REGEN 叙事数据集并提出 CF+内容嵌入经 Adapter 注入 LLM 的融合架构，在 Office/Clothing 两域上 BLEU/ROUGE/语义相似度较单模态提升 4–12%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;多维评测框架&quot;,&quot;候选位置偏差&quot;,&quot;幻觉&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2025/llm_rec/beyond_utility_evaluating_llm_as_recommender/">Beyond Utility: Evaluating LLM as Recommender</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出含历史长度敏感性、候选位置偏差、画像生成与幻觉四维的 LLM 推荐多维评测框架，揭示 LLM 在重排与冷启动占优但位置偏差严重。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2025&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;协同检索增强生成&quot;,&quot;黑盒 LLM 反思重排&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2025/agent_conversational_rec/collaborative_retrieval_for_large_language_model_based_conversational_recom/">Collaborative Retrieval for Large Language Model-based Conversational Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 EASE 协同检索 + 两步 LLM 反思重排为黑盒 LLM 注入 CF 知识，Reddit-v2/Redial 全库排序 Recall@20 显著超越 Zero-shot LLM。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2025&quot;,&quot;多模态推荐与内容理解&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;停留时长预测&quot;,&quot;LLM 语义嵌入&quot;,&quot;评论排序辅助任务&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2025/multimodal_rec/comment_staytime_prediction_with_llm_enhanced_comment_understanding/">Comment Staytime Prediction with LLM-enhanced Comment Understanding</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多模态推荐与内容理解">MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首个评论区停留时长数据集 KuaiComt 与两阶段 LCU 框架：LLM 领域微调 + 评论排序辅助任务，离线全指标提升且线上停留时长 +1.27%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;三元知识库&quot;,&quot;数据为中心&quot;,&quot;知识检索增强&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2025/llm_rec/d2k_turning_historical_data_into_retrievable_knowledge_for_recommender_syst/">D2K: Turning Historical Data into Retrievable Knowledge for Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将历史日志转为用户-物品-上下文三元知识库，Transformer 编码 + 个性化适配注入任意 CTR 模型，AUC 相对提升 1.35%–2.90%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2025&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;可解释推荐&quot;,&quot;情感对齐评估&quot;,&quot;评分条件生成&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2025/agent_conversational_rec/disentangling_likes_and_dislikes_in_personalized_generative_explainable_rec/">Disentangling Likes and Dislikes in Personalized Generative Explainable Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 LLM 重构正负观点分离的可解释推荐数据集，提出情感匹配与正负内容相似度指标，并证明将预测评分作为输入可显著提升情感感知解释生成。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;双源物品索引&quot;,&quot;非侵入对齐重建&quot;,&quot;退火适配器&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2025/llm_rec/eager_llm_enhancing_large_language_models_as_recommenders_through_exogenous/">EAGER-LLM: Enhancing Large Language Models as Recommenders through Exogenous Behavior-Semantic Integration</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以双源知识富集物品索引+非侵入多尺度对齐重建+退火适配器，将外源行为/语义信号注入 LLM 生成式推荐，Amazon 三数据集 Full-Ranking 下 Recall@10 最高提升 26.16%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;分层提示&quot;,&quot;新闻推荐&quot;,&quot;数据增强&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2025/llm_rec/enhancing_news_recommendation_with_hierarchical_llm_prompting/">Enhancing News Recommendation with Hierarchical LLM Prompting</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用分层提示让 LLM 生成更丰富的新闻标题与实体，作为模型无关的数据增强模块，在 MIND 上以 AUC 68.88 超越 GLoCIM 等 SOTA。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;外部知识蒸馏&quot;,&quot;流式分布漂移&quot;,&quot;基础模型&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2025/llm_rec/external_large_foundation_model_how_to_efficiently_serve_trillions_of_param/">External Large Foundation Model: How to Efficiently Serve Trillions of Parameters for Online Ads Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以外部蒸馏+数据增强服务解耦教师训练，用辅助头与学生适配器弥合流式分布漂移，实现万亿参数基础模型零延迟增益服务多业务精排模型。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;不适推荐过滤&quot;,&quot;偏好画像&quot;,&quot;多智能体&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2025/llm_rec/filtering_discomforting_recommendations_with_large_language_models/">Filtering Discomforting Recommendations with Large Language Models</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用可编辑偏好画像+对话式规则生成，以插件方式过滤主观不适推荐；离线代理任务与24人一周用户研究验证有效性。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;GraphRAG&quot;,&quot;可解释推荐&quot;,&quot;知识剪枝&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2025/llm_rec/g_refer_graph_retrieval_augmented_large_language_model_for_explainable_reco/">G-Refer: Graph Retrieval-Augmented Large Language Model for Explainable Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用路径级+节点级混合图检索把协同过滤信号显式翻译为文本，经知识剪枝与 RAFT 微调驱动 LLM 生成可解释推荐，BERT-F1 最高提升 2.81%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;叙事驱动推荐&quot;,&quot;提示策略评测&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2025/llm_rec/large_language_models_as_narrative_driven_recommenders/">Large Language Models as Narrative-Driven Recommenders</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>系统评测 38 个 LLM 在 Reddit 叙事式电影推荐上的表现，发现零样本提示即可显著超越 doc2vec 基线 70%+，中型开源模型接近大型闭源模型。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回+精排&quot;,&quot;端到端联合训练&quot;,&quot;内容编码器&quot;,&quot;推理缓存&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2025/llm_rec/legommenders_a_comprehensive_content_based_recommendation_library_with_llm_/">Legommenders: A Comprehensive Content-Based Recommendation Library with LLM Support</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首个支持内容编码器与行为/交互模块端到端联合训练的推荐库，内置 LLM 编码与数据增强，覆盖 1000+ 模型组合与 15 个数据集，推理缓存带来最高 50x 加速。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;主动推荐&quot;,&quot;影响路径规划&quot;,&quot;用户模拟&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2025/llm_rec/leveraging_llms_for_influence_path_planning_in_proactive_recommendation/">Leveraging LLMs for Influence Path Planning in Proactive Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用提示工程驱动 LLM 零样本规划主动推荐影响路径，配合 LLM 用户模拟器与 SASRec 模拟器双轨评测，显著提升路径连贯性与用户接受度。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;记忆检索&quot;,&quot;困惑度标注&quot;,&quot;长期兴趣建模&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2025/llm_rec/leveraging_memory_retrieval_to_enhance_llm_based_generative_recommendation/">Leveraging Memory Retrieval to Enhance LLM-based Generative Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 PPL 降幅自动标注记忆价值并训练检索器，为 LLM 生成式推荐注入长期兴趣，全库排序下 Recall@1 稳定提升。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2025&quot;,&quot;多任务学习&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;LLM 零样本重排&quot;,&quot;Chain-of-Thought 节点图&quot;,&quot;多准则均衡&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2025/multi_task_reranking/llm4rerank_llm_based_auto_reranking_framework_for_recommendations/">LLM4Rerank: LLM-based Auto-Reranking Framework for Recommendations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将准确性/多样性/公平性抽象为全连接图节点，用零样本 LLM 以 CoT 多跳自动重排，三数据集上多准则均衡超越 SOTA。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2025&quot;,&quot;LLM推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;个性化标题生成&quot;,&quot;风格-内容解耦&quot;,&quot;对比学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2025/llm_rec/panoramic_interests_stylistic_content_aware_personalized_headline_generatio/">Panoramic Interests: Stylistic-Content Aware Personalized Headline Generation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 LLM 协同抽取标题内容与风格双维特征，层次门控融合长短期全景兴趣，指导轻量生成器产出个性化新闻标题，PENS 上 ROUGE-1 达 34.26。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;RAG&quot;,&quot;表征对齐&quot;,&quot;对比学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2025/llm_rec/rallrec_improving_retrieval_augmented_large_language_model_recommendation_w/">RALLRec: Improving Retrieval Augmented Large Language Model Recommendation with Representation Learning</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 LLM 生成物品详述并做文本-协同表征对比对齐，配合时间感知重排器提升 RAG 式 LLM 推荐的检索质量。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2025&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;评测与仿真&quot;,&quot;LLM User Simulator&quot;,&quot;Bounded Rationality&quot;,&quot;角色扮演与可控生成&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2025/agent_conversational_rec/recusersim_a_realistic_and_diverse_user_simulator_for_evaluating_conversati/">RecUserSim: A Realistic and Diverse User Simulator for Evaluating Conversational Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出四模块 LLM Agent 用户模拟器，以有限理性三层 Rating-Action-Response 机制实现真实角色扮演、多样人群覆盖与显式打分，并落地华为 Celia 餐饮助手。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;数据增强/冷启动&quot;,&quot;表格数据合成&quot;,&quot;特征归因重要性采样&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2025/llm_rec/samplellm_optimizing_tabular_data_synthesis_in_recommendations/">SampleLLM: Optimizing Tabular Data Synthesis in Recommendations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>两阶段 LLM 表格数据合成框架：聚类采样示例 + 特征归因重要性采样对齐分布，冷启动场景线上 RPM +2%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;自对弈偏好优化&quot;,&quot;流行度去偏&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2025/llm_rec/sprec_self_play_to_debias_llm_based_recommendation/">SPRec: Self-Play to Debias LLM-based Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>揭示 DPO 反向 KL 的 mode-seeking 本质放大流行度偏差，提出自对弈 SPRec 以模型自身输出作负样本自适应去偏，兼顾准确率与公平性。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;数据压缩/训练加速&quot;,&quot;数据集浓缩&quot;,&quot;提示演化&quot;,&quot;聚类用户合成&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2025/llm_rec/tf_dcon_leveraging_large_language_models_llms_to_empower_training_free_data/">TF-DCon: Leveraging Large Language Models (LLMs) to Empower Training-Free Dataset Condensation for Content-Based Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 ChatGPT 前向式浓缩文本内容与聚类合成假用户，免迭代优化压缩 CBR 数据集 95% 仍保 97% 性能。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;Token Alignment&quot;,&quot;Logits Matching&quot;,&quot;LightGCN&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2025/llm_rec/training_large_recommendation_models_via_graph_language_token_alignment/">Training Large Recommendation Models via Graph-Language Token Alignment</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将 LightGCN 用户/物品节点经投影器对齐进 LLaMA-2 词表空间，用 GLLM logits 匹配层做端到端物品预测，Full-Ranking 下 NDCG@10 最高提升 185%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;不确定性量化&quot;,&quot;不确定性分解&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2025/llm_rec/uncertainty_quantification_and_decomposition_for_llm_based_recommendation/">Uncertainty Quantification and Decomposition for LLM-based Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 Plackett-Luce 单次推理估计列表排序预测熵，并分解为推荐/提示不确定性，指导不确定性感知提示提升 N@20 约 17%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2025&quot;,&quot;跨域与多场景推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;LLM 零样本重排&quot;,&quot;跨域知识迁移&quot;,&quot;提示工程&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2025/cross_domain_multi_scenario/uncovering_cross_domain_recommendation_ability_of_large_language_models/">Uncovering Cross-Domain Recommendation Ability of Large Language Models</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="跨域与多场景推荐">Cross</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用源域购买序列+跨域引导提示驱动 LLM 零样本重排目标域候选，揭示域间隙与模型规模对跨域迁移的决定性影响。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2025&quot;,&quot;自监督对比学习与交互去噪&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;知识蒸馏&quot;,&quot;信息瓶颈&quot;,&quot;图 CoT 推理&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2025/self_supervised_denoising/unleashing_the_power_of_large_language_model_for_denoising_recommendation/">Unleashing the Power of Large Language Model for Denoising Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>LLaRD 用 LLM 生成偏好知识与图 CoT 关系知识，再以信息瓶颈对齐去噪图，Full-Ranking 下 Recall@20 最高提升约 14%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2026&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;LLM Agent&quot;,&quot;替代互补关系推理&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2026/agent_conversational_rec/agentdr_dynamic_recommendation_with_implicit_item_item_relations_via_llm_ba/">AgentDR: Dynamic Recommendation with Implicit Item-Item Relations via LLM-based Agents</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将全库排序委托给传统推荐工具，用 LLM Agent 做个性化工具加权与替代/互补关系重排，全排序平均提升约两倍。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2026&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;Agentic Benchmark&quot;,&quot;Checklist-driven Evaluation&quot;,&quot;Personalization&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2026/agent_conversational_rec/agenticshop_benchmarking_agentic_product_curation_for_personalized_web_shop/">AgenticShop: Benchmarking Agentic Product Curation for Personalized Web Shopping</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首个开放网络个性化商品策展基准，用真实购买史构建叙事画像与六维清单，LLM-as-a-judge 可验证评估，揭示现有智能体策展得分仅 15–38%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;无用户嵌入&quot;,&quot;对比学习增强&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2026/llm_rec/alphafree_recommendation_free_from_users_ids_and_gnns/">AlphaFree: Recommendation Free from Users, IDs, and GNNs</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用语言表征替代ID、在线推断用户偏好、以相似物品增强+对比对齐替代GNN，实现无用户/无ID/无GNN的轻量推荐，内存降69%、冷启动提升26–38%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2026&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;流行度偏差&quot;,&quot;提示工程&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/bridging_semantic_understanding_and_popularity_bias_with_llms/">Bridging Semantic Understanding and Popularity Bias with LLMs</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将流行度偏差拆解为物品侧与用户侧语义，用结构化指令提示引导 RecLLM 双侧去偏，在 LtC/MRMC 与 MRR/F1 上同时超越多样性提示基线。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2026&quot;,&quot;强化学习与动态探索推荐&quot;,&quot;出价与预算分配&quot;,&quot;LLM 上下文学习&quot;,&quot;GRPO 微调&quot;,&quot;双阶段智能体&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2026/rl_bandit_rec/dara_few_shot_budget_allocation_in_online_advertising_via_in_context_decisi/">DARA: Few-shot Budget Allocation in Online Advertising via In-Context Decision Making with RL-Finetuned LLMs</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>双阶段 LLM 智能体分离少样本规划与精细优化，配合动态参考策略 GRPO-Adaptive 微调，在真实与合成广告环境中显著降低边际 ROI 方差。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2026&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;连续Token分词&quot;,&quot;条件扩散生成&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2026/generative_rec/diffusion_generative_recommendation_with_continuous_tokens/">Diffusion Generative Recommendation with Continuous Tokens</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 σ-VAE 连续分词器替代量化码本，并以条件扩散+离散散度损失生成用户偏好，四数据集 Full-Ranking 上 HR@10 平均超 TIGER 11.76%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2026&quot;,&quot;跨域与多场景推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;联邦学习&quot;,&quot;LoRA 方向分解&quot;,&quot;个性化聚合权重&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2026/cross_domain_multi_scenario/fedecider_an_llm_based_framework_for_federated_cross_domain_recommendation/">FeDecider: An LLM-Based Framework for Federated Cross-Domain Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="跨域与多场景推荐">Cross</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>联邦跨域 LLM 推荐中，服务器只广播 LoRA 更新的方向分量以消除尺度噪声，客户端用可学习个性化权重做数据感知融合，Goodreads 平均 Hit@5 提升 3.8%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;推理蒸馏&quot;,&quot;对比自蒸馏&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2026/llm_rec/from_reasoning_llms_to_bert_a_two_stage_distillation_framework_for_search_r/">From Reasoning LLMs to BERT: A Two-Stage Distillation Framework for Search Relevance</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>美团搜索广告两阶段框架：领域自适应推理LLM做教师标注，CRSD自蒸馏将推理能力内化进6层BERT，线上AdCTR+0.91%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2026&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;LLM 语义嵌入&quot;,&quot;行为校准&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2026/sequential_rec/generative_archetype_grounded_item_representations_for_sequential_recommend/">Generative Archetype-Grounded Item Representations for Sequential Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 LLM 生成物品“目标人群原型”文本并单次前向抽取 prefill/decoding 双嵌入，再以行为关联分数调制排斥力做校准，Full-Ranking 下 HR@10 最高 +6.95%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;时间间隔预测&quot;,&quot;上下文提示&quot;,&quot;零样本推理&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2026/llm_rec/is_more_context_always_better_examining_llm_reasoning_capability_for_time_i/">Is More Context Always Better? Examining LLM Reasoning Capability for Time Interval Prediction</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>系统评测 GPT-4o/Claude/Gemini 在复购时间间隔预测上的零样本能力，发现 LLM 仅胜统计基线、全面落后 XGBoost，且中等上下文最优、高上下文反降。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;语义推理&quot;,&quot;群体隐式兴趣&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2026/llm_rec/iterative_semantic_reasoning_from_individual_to_group_interests_for_generat/">Iterative Semantic Reasoning from Individual to Group Interests for Generative Recommendation with LLMs</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 LLM 双向 CoT 推理物品/用户语义，构建个体显式兴趣图与相似用户群隐式兴趣图，并以对比蒸馏迭代对齐，直接推荐 H@10 最高提升 24.57%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2026&quot;,&quot;冷启动&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;LLM Reasoning&quot;,&quot;GRPO&quot;,&quot;Cold-Start&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2026/cold_start_rec/llm_reasoning_for_cold_start_item_recommendation/">LLM Reasoning for Cold-Start Item Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>在 Netflix 冷启动重排任务上系统对比结构化推理、软自洽与 SFT/GRPO 混合微调，推理微调模型在 Discovery 指标上超越线上生产排序模型最高 8%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2026&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;LLM多智能体协作&quot;,&quot;动态编排&quot;,&quot;协同过滤&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2026/agent_conversational_rec/multi_agent_collaborative_filtering_orchestrating_users_and_items_for_agent/">Multi-Agent Collaborative Filtering: Orchestrating Users and Items for Agentic Recommendations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将相似用户与相关物品实例化为LLM智能体，由中心编排器动态招募并下发个性化指令，多轮讨论聚合协同信号，三域HR@10最高提升17.9%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2026&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;联邦持续个性化&quot;,&quot;软提示与原型锚定&quot;,&quot;差分隐私&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/prototype_aligned_federated_soft_prompts_for_continual_web_personalization/">Prototype-Aligned Federated Soft-Prompts for Continual Web Personalization</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>冻结骨干上注入双时间尺度软提示，长期提示锚定DP联邦原型库，八基准NDCG@10平均+2.9%且降低遗忘与适应步数。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;偏好漂移&quot;,&quot;区域级知识编辑&quot;,&quot;区域专属 LoRA&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2026/llm_rec/raie_region_aware_incremental_preference_editing_with_lora_for_llm_based_re/">RAIE: Region-Aware Incremental Preference Editing with LoRA for LLM-based Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>冻结 LLM 主干，用球面 k-means 构建偏好区域并为每区配专属 LoRA，通过 Update/Expand/Add 三种区域级编辑与置信路由实现抗遗忘的增量偏好适配。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2026&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;用户模拟&quot;,&quot;指令遵循推荐&quot;,&quot;多轮强化学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2026/agent_conversational_rec/recoworld_building_simulated_environments_for_agentic_recommender_systems/">RecoWorld: Building Simulated Environments for Agentic Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出双视角LLM仿真环境，用户模拟器在多轮交互中发出反思指令，驱动指令遵循推荐器优化留存，并给出三种历史建模方案与多智能体群体仿真蓝图。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;规则感知奖励塑形&quot;,&quot;自适应引导回放&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2026/llm_rec/taosr_agrl_adaptive_guided_reinforcement_learning_framework_for_e_commerce_/">TaoSR-AGRL: Adaptive Guided Reinforcement Learning Framework for E-commerce Search Relevance</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用规则感知稠密奖励塑形与自适应引导回放缓解 GRPO 奖励稀疏，淘宝搜索相关性 Macro-F1 提升 2.28pt 并上线。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;思维链蒸馏&quot;,&quot;LoRA 专家融合&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2026/llm_rec/thinkrec_thinking_based_recommendation_via_llm/">ThinkRec: Thinking-based Recommendation via LLM</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以合成推理链激活 LLM 的 System 2 思维，并用实例级 LoRA 专家融合适配异构用户，AUC 平均提升 7.96%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;协同信号对齐&quot;,&quot;软标签NTP&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2026/llm_rec/token_level_collaborative_alignment_for_llm_based_generative_recommendation/">Token-level Collaborative Alignment for LLM-based Generative Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将CF物品级logits投影为token级分布并与one-hot融合成软标签，以soft-NTP损失显式对齐LLM生成，即插即用提升多骨干推荐效果。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;语义ID&quot;,&quot;强化学习推理&quot;,&quot;电商搜索&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2026/generative_rec/towards_context_aware_reasoning_enhanced_generative_searching_in_e_commerce/">Towards Context-aware Reasoning-enhanced Generative Searching in E-commerce</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将电商搜索异构上下文统一为JSON文本与残差K-Means语义ID，用SFT-RL自演化后训练与去偏R-GRPO提升生成式搜索排序，All-100K上HR@10相对提升6.24%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2026&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;Machine Unlearning&quot;,&quot;Influence Function&quot;,&quot;LLM4Rec Fairness&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/towards_fair_large_language_model_based_recommender_systems_without_costly_/">Towards Fair Large Language Model-based Recommender Systems without Costly Retraining</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将 LLM-RS 去偏重构为免重训机器遗忘任务：可学习掩码定位致偏样本 + 影响函数一步参数修正，公平性提升同时精度不降、耗时降 96%。</p>
</dd>
</dl>
